Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any biodiversity module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 124. The projective resolution has length at most 49 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_124(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 124. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^49(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the biodiversity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 577. The moduli space has dimension at most 384 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on biodiversity structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 577. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 384. QED.
